2a7(dkon brtsegs): bya'i tshogs rnams gnas te/de dag kyang sangs rgyas kyi mthus/'dod chags kyi gnod pa med do//zhe sdang dang /gti mug gi gnod pa med do//gcig la gcig mi za'o//de dag gcig la gcig 
2a8(dkon brtsegs): pha mar 'du shes shing gnas so//yang ri'i rgyal po bya rgod kyi phung po de la/shing gi tshogs brgya stong du ma yal ga sdug cing /lo ma rgyas la 'dab na/drang srong gnas pa dag yod pa la 'di lta ste/
2b1(dkon brtsegs): thang shing dang /blag sha dang /a shwad tha dang /bi bi ling gi shing dang /rgya skegs shing dang /ha ri ta dang /a ru ra'i shing dang /ba ru ra'i shing dang /bA ru ra'i shing dang /sA lA dang /tA la dang /nag tam la dang /

2b3(dkon brtsegs): kung dza ka dang /rta ba ka dang /sa ra la dang /el dang /k+Shu ba dang /n+ya gro dang /dri D+ha ni ma dang /'bra go dang /kam bi la dang /bal sha dang /ni yam dang /ka ke ri ya dang /
2b4(dkon brtsegs): ni da t+ya bi lo ti dang /bog dkar po'i shing dang /sra rtsi pog gi shing dang /shing kun shing dang /tsan dan dang /tsan dan dmar po dang /u pa ta dang /sa ka bi rtsa dang /gardza dang /bi gar rdza dang /
2b5(dkon brtsegs): gser shing dang /pa na sa dang /rgun shing dang /shing bal shing dang /pa ri sA tra dang /hil ste/lha dang bcas pa dag go/yang ri'i rgyal po bya rgod gyi phung po de la thang las skyes pa'i me tog rnam 
2b6(dkon brtsegs): pa mang po dag skye ba 'di lta ste/i ti mug ta ga dang /tsam pa ka dang /skya snar dang /bar shi ka dang /sna ma'i me tog dang /dri mchog dang /yu ti ka dang /da nu ska ri dang /bal la da nga 
2b7(dkon brtsegs): ta ra ni dang /gab ta ra ni dang /kang sa gun dang /lug mig dang /ha ri ni ka dang /mad ta li ka dang /man dzu bu spi ka ste/me tog sna tshogs de lta bu dag gis/ri'i rgyal po bya rgod kyi phung po gang zhing 
2b8(dkon brtsegs): mdzes so//yang ri'i rgyal po bya rgo da kyi phung po de la/chu las skyes pa'i me tog rna ma pa mang po dag skye ba 'di blta ste/ud bal dang /pad+ma dang /ku mu ta dang /pad+ma dkar po dang /pad ga ni

3b1(dkon brtsegs): bar sha ka'i me tog gi dri bzhin no//'di lta ste/dper na/ka tsa lin da'i gos la reg pa bzhin te/rkang mthil bzhan na ni sor bzhis nems shes byed pa/rkang mthil bteg na ni sor bzhis bar ces 
3b2(dkon brtsegs): byed pa/me tog dang /'dab ma dang 'bras bu dang ldan pa/lta bas chog mi shes pa dag go/yang ri'i rgyal po bya rgod kyi phung po de la/'bab chu'i ngos dang /mtsho bran dang /mtsho dang /mtshe'u dang /
3b3(dkon brtsegs): rdzing rnam pa mang po bsil ba'i chus rab tu gang ba dag yod de/de dag las pad ma sngon po dang /ser po dang /dmar po dang /dkar po dang /leb rgan dang /shel gyi kha dog lta bu dang /me'i kha dog lta bu dang /
3b4(dkon brtsegs): gser gyi kha dog lta bu dang /che byad shing rta 'a'i phar lo tsam dag skyes te/de dag bcad na dri ngad phyogs bzhi kun tu dpag tshad tsam du lngar ngo //ri'i rgyal po de'i rtse mo la pad ma'i gdan chen po an d+ha rnyil gzhan gyis mi tshugs pa/
3b5(dkon brtsegs): rdo rje lta bu'i chu ba can/bai dUr+ya chen pos mtha' bskor ba/'dzam bu'i chu bo'i gser dri ma med pa'i 'dab ma rgyas pa/tsan dan dus kyi rjes su 'brang ba'i snying po rnam par dag pa/rin po che rdo'i snying po'i ze ba dang 
3b6(dkon brtsegs): ldan pa/tshang rgya mtsho ltar yangs shing rgya che ba/lha ma yin gyi dbang po sa yas snying po kun tu bzung ba nor bu rin po che sa ya'i dra ba sna tshogs kyis g.yogs pa/klu'i dbang po sa yas sprin las spos chu'i char yang phab pa/
3b7(dkon brtsegs): nam mkha' lding gi dbang po sa yas kha nas dar sbyangs shing ri mo byas pa/ma 'am ci'i dbang po sa yas/phan pa'i sems kyis bltas pa/lto 'phye chen po'i dbang po sa yas/gdong gis phyag 'tshal zhing ri mo byas pa/
3b8(dkon brtsegs): dri za'i dbang po sa yas bstod cing bsngags la mchod pa/lha'i dbang po sa yas/me tog dang /phreng ba dang /spos dang /byug pa dang /phye ma dang /gos dang /gdugs dang /rgyal mtshan dang /lha'i ba dan gyi sprin gyi char phab pa/tshangs 
4a1(dkon brtsegs): @#//pa'i dbang po sa yas mgo bos phyag 'tshal zhing ri mo byas pa/gnas gtsang ma'i ris kyi lha'i bu sa yas thal mo sbyar nas phyag byas pa/mi'i dbang po 'khor los sgyur pa sa yas nor bu rin po che sna bdun dang ldan pas bsu ba/rgya mtsho'i 
4a2(dkon brtsegs): lha sa yas bsu zhing phyag byas pa/nor bu rin po che skar mdog sa yas 'od zer gyis snang bar byas pa/bsod nams kyis dag pa'i nor bu rin po che sa yas/shin tu nges par mdzes par byas pa/nor bu rin po  che rnam par snang byed sa yas 
4a3(dkon brtsegs): snying po dri ma med pa/nor bu rin po che dpal gyi 'od zer sa yas dpal rab tu thal le pa/nor bu rin po che'i mdzod sna tshogs sa yas mtha' yas par snang ba nor bu rin po che 'dzam bu'i rgyal mtshan sa yas legs par bzung bas brtan 
4a4(dkon brtsegs): par gyur pa/nor bu rin po che rdo rje seng ge sa yas gzhan gyis mi thub pa bkod pa/nor bu rin po cheti ma'i snying po sa yas rgya cher spras pa/nor bu rin po chebasam gyis mi khyab pa sa yas kha dog sna tshogs kyis ri mo byas pa yid bzhin gyi 
4a5(dkon brtsegs): nor bu rin po ches yas mi zad pa bkod pas ri mo byas pa gzhan gyis mi thub pa'i rdo rje lta bu'i yu ba can gyi biTUr+ya chen pos bskor ba/dri ma med pa'i 'dzam bu chu bo'i gser gyi 'dab ma rgyas pa/in+d+ra ni ya'i burma chen po'i gdan byung ste/pad+ma chen po deni/de bzhin gshegs pa'i 'jig rten las 
4a6(dkon brtsegs): 'das pa'i dge ba'i rtsa ba las nges par byung ba/byang chub sems dpa'i bsam pas mnos pa/phyogs thams cad du mngon du go par byed pa sgyu mar gyur pa'i chos las nges par byung ba/dri nga ba med pa'i las las skyes pa/nyon mongs 
4a7(dkon brtsegs): pa med pa'i chos nyid kyis tshul bkod pa/rmi lam lta bu'i chos nyid kyis kun tu rgyu ba/mngon par 'du byed pa med pa'i chos nyid kyi rgyas btab pa/chags pa med pa dang ldan pa/phyogs kun tu chos kyi dbyings kyis khyab par byed pa     
4a8(dkon brtsegs): sangs rgyas kyi yul rab tu brjod pa dang 'thun pa ste/de'i rnam pa dang /yon tan dang /dbyibs dang /kha dog dang /bkod pa'i mtha' ni bskal pa grangs med par yang rtog par mi nus pa'i pad ma chen po de la/bcom ldan 'das 
4b1(dkon brtsegs): skyil mo krung bcas nas bzhugs te/'di ltar dge slong brgya stong gi dge slong gi dge 'dun chen po dang /thabs gcig pa la 'di lta ste/tshe dang ldan pa kun shes ko'u dri c+ya dang /tshe dang ldan pa rta thul dang /tshe dang ldan pa rlangs pa dang /tshe dang ldan pa'i ming chen dang /tshe dang ldan pa 'char ka 
4b2(dkon brtsegs): dang /tshe dang ldan pa grags pa dang /tshe dang ldanapa gang po dang /tshe dang ldan pa dri ma med pa dang /tshe dang ldan pas lang bdag dang /tshe dang ldan pa lag bzangs dang /tshe dang ldan pa ldeng rgyas 'od srung dang /tshe dang ldan pa chu klung 'od srung dang /tshe dngaldan pa g.ya 'od 
4b3(dkon brtsegs): srung dang /tshe dang ldan ba 'od srung chen po dang /tshe dang ldan bkatA'i bu dang /tshe dang ldan pa shA ra dwa ti'i bu dang /tshe dang ldanapa mo'u dgal gyi bu chen po dang tshe dang ldan pa ma 'gags pa dang /tshe dang lda na par ba 'byor dang /tshe dang ldan pa nam gru dang /tshe dang ldan pa
4b4(dkon brtsegs): byams ma'i bu gang po dang /tshe dang lan banye bar 'khor dang tshe dang ldan pa sgra can zin dang /tshe dang ldan pakun dga' bo la sogs pa dge slong brgyad stong thams cad kyang /bden pa'i ngo bo nyid kyi tshul mngon par rdzogs par rtogs pa/yang dag pa'i mtha' 
4b5(dkon brtsegs): mngon gsum du gyur pa/chos kyi rang bzhin la zhugs pa/srid pa'i rgya mtsho las rgal ba/de bzhin nyid nam mkha' lta bu'i spyod yul pa/kun tu sbyor ba dang /bag la nyal gyi 'ching ba bcad pa/gnas dang brten la ma chags pa 
4b6(dkon brtsegs): nam mkha' ltar zhi ba la rnam par rgyu ba/sangs rgyas la nem nur dang /yid gnyis dang /the tsom za ba bcad pa sangs rgyas kyi ye shes kyi rgya mtsho la mos pa'i lam du zhugs pa/'gro ba thams cad la phan pa'i phyir zhugs 
4b7(dkon brtsegs): pa/gsol ba ma btab par dge ba'i bshes gnyen byed pa/sems can thams cad bsrung bar zhugs pa/sems can thams can yongs su mi gtong ba'i mdza' bshes su gyur pa/sangs rgyas kyi bstan pa'i 
4b8(dkon brtsegs): spyod yul las nges par byung ba/de bzhin gshegs pa'i bstan pa bsrung bar bzhugs pa/sangs rgyas kyi bstan ba kun tu 'dzin par smon lam btab pa/de bzhin gshegs pa'i gdung rigs 'byung bar mngon tu phyogs pa 
5a1(dkon brtsegs): @#//thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes mngon du gyur pa sha stag go/kun tu bzang po dang /byang chub sems dpa' 'jam dpal la sogs pa/byang chub sems dpa' brgyad stong dang thabs gcig pa la 
5a2(dkon brtsegs): 'di lta ste/byang chub sems dpa' ye shes bla ma'i ye shes can dang /rin chen bla ma'i ye shes can dang skad thams cad shes pa can dang /thogs pa med pa'i ye shes can dang /me tog bla ma'i ye shes can dang//
5a3(dkon brtsegs): nyi ma'i bla ma'i ye shes can dang/zla ba'i bla ma'i ye shes can dag/dri ma med pa'i bla ma'i ye shes can dang rdo rje'i ye shes can dang rdul med ye shes can dang /byang chub sems pa'i rnam 
5a4(dkon brtsegs): par snang ba'i ye shes can dang byang chub sems dpa' snang ba'i rgyal mtshan dang /lhun po rgyal mtshan dang /rin chen rgyal mtshan dang /ma chags rgyal mtshan dang /me tog rgyal mtshan dang /dri 
5a5(dkon brtsegs): med rgyal mtshan dang /nyi ma'i rgyal mtshan dang /mdzes pa'i rgyal mtshan dang /rdul med rgyal mtshan dang /byang chub sems dpa' rnam par snang ba'i rgyal mtshan dang/byang chub sems dpa'
5a6(dkon brtsegs): sa'i gzi brjid dang /rin po che'i gzi brjid dang /gzi brjid chen po dang /ye shes rdo rje'i gzi brjid dang /dri ma med pa'i gzi brjid dang /nyi ma'i gzi brjid dang /zla ba'i gzi brjid dang /bsod 
5a7(dkon brtsegs): nams ri'i gzi brjid dang /ye shes snang ba'i gzi brjid dang/byang chub sems pa kun nas dpal gyi gzi brjid dang /byang chub sems gda' nam mkha'i snying po dang /pad ma'i snying po dang /
5a8(dkon brtsegs): rin po che'i snying po dang /nyi ma'i snying po dang /yon tan rnam par dag pa'i snying po dang /chos rgya mtsho'i snying po dang /rnam par snang ba'i snying po dang /lte 
5b1(dkon brtsegs): ba'i snying po dang /byang chub sems dpa' pad ma'i dpal gyi snying po dang /byang chub kyi sems dpa' nyi ma'i mig dang /rnam par dag pa'i mig dang /dri ma med pa'i mig dang /ma chags pa'i mig dang /
5b2(dkon brtsegs): kun tu lta ba'i mig dang /ye shes shin tu rno ba'i mig dang /rdo rje'i mig dang /rin po che'i mig dang /nam mkha'i mig dang /byang chub sems pa'i kun nas mig dang /byang chub sem dpa' 
5b3(dkon brtsegs): lha'i cod pan dang /chos kyi dbyings snang ba'i nor bu'i cod pan dang /byang chub kyi snying po'i nor bu'i cod pan dang phyogs rnam par snang ba'i cod pan dang /sangs rgyas thams cad byung ba'i snying 
5b4(dkon brtsegs): po'i cod pan dang /'jig rten thams cad las 'phags pa'i cod pan dang /kun nas rnam par snang ba'i cod dpan dang /zil gyis mi gnon pa'i cod pan dang /de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi 
5b5(dkon brtsegs): seng ge'i gdan g.yogs pa'i cod pan dang /byang chu sems dpa' nam mkha' lta bu'i chos kyi dbyings kun nas snang ba'i cod pan dang byang chub sems dpa' tshangs pa'i dbang po'i gtsug phud dang /klu'i 
5b6(dkon brtsegs): dbang po'i gtsug phud dang sangs rgyas thams cad kyi sprul pa snang ba'i gtsug phud dang /byang chub kyi snying bo'i gtsug phud dang /smon lam thams cad kyi rgya mtsho'i dbyings kyi nor bu'i 
5b7(dkon brtsegs): rgyal po'i gtsug phud dang de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi 'od kyi dkyil 'khor gyis 'grol ba'i nor bu rin po che rab tu bsgrags pa'i gtsug phud dang /nam mkha'i dkyil 'khor thams cad 
5b8(dkon brtsegs): dbyer med pa rnam par rig pa'i nor bu rin po ches brgyan pa'i gtsug phud dang /de bzhin gshegs thams cad kyi rnam par sprul pa snang ba'i rgyal mtshan gyi nor bu rin po che'i dra bas g.yogs pa'i 
6a1(dkon brtsegs): @#//gtsug phud dang /de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi chos kyi 'khor lo'i dbyangs kyi gtsug phud dang /byang chub sems dpa' dus gsum thams cad kyi 'khor lo'i dbyibs 
6a2(dkon brtsegs): kyi gtsug phud ces bya ba dang /byang chub sems pa' 'od chen dang /dri ma med pa'i 'od dang /rin po che'i 'od dang /rdul med 'od dang /skar ma'i 'od dang /chos kyi 'od dang /zhi ba'i 'od dang /
6a3(dkon brtsegs): nyi ma'i 'od dang /rnam par sprul pa'i 'od dang /byang chub sems dpa' lha'i 'od dang /byang chub sems dpa' bsod nams kyi tog dang /rto ye shes kyi tog dang /chos kyi tog dang /mngon 
6a4(dkon brtsegs): par shes pa'i tog dang /'od kyi tog dang /me tog gi tog dang /nor bu'i tog dang /blo'i tog dang /tshangs pa'i tog dang /byang chub sems dba' kun nas snang ba'i tog dang /byang chub sems
6a5(dkon brtsegs): dpa' tshangs pa'i dbyangs dang /rgya mtsho'i dbyangs dang /sa nges par sgrog pa'i dbyangs dang /'jig rten dbang po'i dbyangs dang /ri'i dbang po'i dbyangs dang /ri'i dbang po'i rgyal po kun tu rdob 
6a6(dkon brtsegs): pa'i dbyangs dang /chos kyi dbyings rgyas par 'gengs pa'i dbyangs dang /chos kyi rgya mtsho thams cad bsgrags nga'i dbyangs dang /bdud kyi dkyil 'khor thams cad rab tu 'joms pa'i dbyangs dang /snying 
6a7(dkon brtsegs): rje chen po'i tshul gyi 'brug nges par bsgrags pa'i dbyangs dang /byang chub sems dpa' 'gro ba thams cad kyi sdug bsngal rab tu zhi ba'i sgra dbyangs dang /byang chub sems dpa' chos 'phags 
6a8(dkon brtsegs): dang /khyad par 'phags dang /ye shes 'phags dang /bsod nams ri rab 'phags dang /yon tan mchog rab 'phags dang /grags pa 'phags dang /kun nas snang 'phags dang /byams
6b1(dkon brtsegs): pa chen po 'phags dang /ye shes snang ba 'phags dang /byang chub sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs pa'i gdung rigs 'phags dang /byang chub kyi sems dpa' 'od dpal dang /mchog rab dpal 
6b2(dkon brtsegs): dang /chos 'phags dpal dang /rnam par snang ba'i dpal dang /chos dpal dang /zla ba dpal dang /nam mkha' dpal dang /rin chen dpal dang /tog dpal dang /byang chub sems
6b3(dkon brtsegs): dpa' ye shes dpal dang /byang chub kyi semasad pa' sa la'i dbang po'i rgyal po dang /chos kyi dbang po'i rgyal po dang/glang po che'i dbang po'i rgyal po dang tshangs pa'i dbang po'i rgyal po dang
6b4(dkon brtsegs): dbang po'i rgyal po dang tshogs kyi dbang po'i rgyal po dag lha'i dbang po'i rgyal po dan zhi ba'i dbang po'i rgyal po da mi g.yo ba'i dbang po'i rgyal po de khyu mchog gi dbang po'i rgyal
6b5(dkon brtsegs): po dang /byang chub sems pa' byang chub mchog gi dbang po'i rgyal po dang byang chub sems dpa' zhi ba'i sgra dang chags pa med pa'i sgra dang sa'i dbyangs sgra dang rgya mtsho'i dbyangs
6b6(dkon brtsegs): sgra dang sprin gyi dbyangs sgra dang chos snang ba'i sgra dang nam mkha'i dbyangs sgra dang byang chub sems dpa'i dge ba'i rtsa ba thams cad nges par bsgrags pa'i sgra dang sngon gyi smon lam
6b7(dkon brtsegs): gyis yang dag par bskul ba'i sgra dang byang chub sems pa'i bdud kyi dkyil 'khor thams cad kyi dbyangs sgra dang byang chub kyi sems dpa' ye shes lhun po'i blo dang nam mkha'i blo dang dri ma med
6b8(dkon brtsegs): pa'i blo dang chags pa med pa'i blo dang sad pa'i blo dang dus gsum snang ba'i blo dang rin po che'i blo dang yangs pa'i blo dang kun nas snang ba'i blo dang byang chub sems dpa chos kyi
7a1(dkon brtsegs): @#//dbyings kyi tshul /snang ba'i blo la sogs pa/byang chub sems dpa' brgyad stong thams cad kyang /kun tu bzang po'i spyod pa'i smon lam las nges par byung ba/sangs rgyas kyi
7a2(dkon brtsegs): zhing thams cad rgyas par 'gengs pas/spyod yul thog+sa pa med pa/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi thad du 'gro baslus mtha' yas par byin gyis rlob pa/sangs rgyas kyi rnam par sprul
7a3(dkon brtsegs): pa thams cad rnam par rig pa/mtha' yas pa la dmigs pa'i/mig gi dkyil 'khor rnam par dag pa/de bzhin gshegs pathams cad mngon par rdzogs par byang chub pa'i sgor 'gro ba rgyun/
7a4(dkon brtsegs): mi gcod pas/'gro ba tshad med pa/chos rgya mtsho thams cad kyi tshul shes pa/snang ba nod pas snang ba mtha' yas pa/so so yang dag par rig pa rnam par dag pas/bskal pa mtha' yas par/
7a5(dkon brtsegs): yon tan mi zad par ston pa//ye shes kyi spyod yul rnam par dag pas/nam mkha'i khams kyis klas pa/bsam pa ji lta ba bzhin du 'gro ba'i lus gzugs
7a6(dkon brtsegs): kun tu ston pas gnas pa med pa/sems can med pa dang ldan pa'i dbyings yongs su shes pas rab rib med pa/chos kyi dbyings thams cad 'od zer gyi dra bas rgyas par 'gengs pas/shes
7a7(dkon brtsegs): rab nam mkha' lta bu/sems shin tu zhi zhing rab tu zhi ba/gzungs kyi rigs thams cad shes pa'i yul dang ldan pa/ting nge 'dzin gyis pha rol gnon pa la 'jigs pa med pa/chos kyi dbying
7a8(dkon brtsegs): sa gnas pa'i mthar thug pa'i mig can/chos thams cad mi dmigs par gnas pa thob pa/shes rab rgya mtsho mtha' yas pas rnam par rgyu ba/ye shes dam pa'i pha rol tu son pa/shes
7b1(dkon brtsegs): rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa thob pa/rdzu 'phrul gyi pha rol tu phyin pas/'gro ba mchog thams cad kyi pha rol tu son pa/ting nge 'dzin mchog la dbang thob pa sha stag go/dge slong ma lnga brgya dang yang thabs gcig pa 
7b2(dkon brtsegs): la 'di lta ste/go'u ta mi'i skye dgu'i bdag mo chen mo dang /dge slong ma bde ma dang /dge slong ma ud pa la'i mdog dang /dge slong ma go'u ta ma'i skem mo dang /skrag can zin gyi ma dge slong ma grags 'dzin 
7b3(dkon brtsegs): la sogs pa/dge slong ma lnga brgya dang ya thabs gcig go/dge bsnyen lnga brgya tsam dang thabs gcig pa la 'di lta ste/dge bsnyen gzi brjid bzang po dang /sa'i gzi brjid dang /shes rab 'od dang 
7b4(dkon brtsegs): grags pa'i gzi brjid dang /grags mchog gzi brjid dang /blo gros bzang dang /zla ba'i dpal dang /zla ba dga' dang /cher dga' dang /nga skrag can bzang dang /cher bzang la sogs pa 
7b5(dkon brtsegs): dge bsnyen lnga brgya dang yang thabs cig go/dge bsnyen ma lnga brgya tsam la 'di lta ste/dge bsnyenam 'od chen dang /'od bzang sa dang /lus bzang sa dang /mngon dga' lus dang /bzang mo 
7b6(dkon brtsegs): dang /bzang dpal dang /zla 'od dang /tog 'od dang /dpal 'od dang /mig bzangs la sogs pa dge bsnyen ma lnga brgya dang yang thabs gcig go/de bzhin du lha dang klu dang /gnod sbyin dang 
7b7(dkon brtsegs): dri za dang /lha ma yin dang /nam mkha' lding dang /mi'am ci dang /lto 'phye chen po rnams kyis yongs su bskor cing mdun gyis bltas nas/bcom ldan 'das chos ston te/'di lta ste 
7b8(dkon brtsegs): sdom pa gsum bstan pa zhes bya ba'i chos kyi sgo'i le'u yang dag par rab tu ston to//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi yul/byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa rab tu brjod pa/chos kyi dbyings kyi 
8a1(dkon brtsegs): @#//dkyil 'khor thams cad snang ba/chos kyi dkyil 'khor thams cad yang dag par 'du bar snang ba/zhing gi dkyil 'khor thams cad rnam par spyod ba'i rgyan/phas kyi rgol ba thams
8a2(dkon brtsegs): cad rnam par 'thor ba/bdud kyi rtsod pa'i pha rol gyi dmag tshogs rab tu 'joms pa/sems can gyi khams thams cad tshim par byed pa/sems can thams cad kyi sems kyi thabis po snang bar byed pa/
8a3(dkon brtsegs): sems can thams cad kyi bsam pa ji lta ba bzhin du go bar byed pa/sems can thabs cad kyi dbang po bzhin du 'khor lo skor bar snang ba rab tu ston to//de nas tshe dang ldan pa 'od srung chen po stan las lang+sa 
8a4(dkon brtsegs): te/bla gos phrag pa gcig tu bgos nas/pus mo g.yas pa'i lha nga sa la btsugs te/bcom ldan 'das ga la ba der logs su thal mo sbyar ba btud nas/bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to//
8a5(dkon brtsegs): bcom ldan 'das sems can gang dag sangs rgyas kyi chos tshol ba dang/sangs rgyas kyi stobs dang/mi 'jigs pa tshol ba de dag gis/chos ji lta bu dag gcig yang dag par blangs te gnas par bgyi
8a6(dkon brtsegs): de dag yongs su smin par 'gyur ba'i chos rnams ni ji lta bu zhig lags /de dag bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub las slar mi ldog cing/thob par 'gyur ba'i chos rnamasani ji lta 
8a7(dkon brtsegs): bu zhig lags//de skad ces gsol pa dang/bcom ldan 'das kyis tshe dang ldan pa 'od sru chen po la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to//'od srung khyod skye bo mang po la phan pa dang/skye bo
8a8(dkon brtsegs): mang po la bde ba dang'jig rten la snying brtse ba dang/lha dang /mi rnams kyi don dang /phan pa dang/bde ba'i phyir zhugs te/'di lta bu'i gnas dag 'dri bar sems pa ni/'od srung khyod legs so//
8b1(dkon brtsegs): legs so//'od srung de'i phyir legs par rab tu nyon la yid la zung shig dang bshad do//tshe dang ldan pa 'od srungs chen po dang /thams cad dang ldan pa'i 'khor des/bcom ldan 'das kyi ltar 
8b2(dkon brtsegs): nyan pa dang /bcom ldan 'das kyis de dag la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to//'od srung sems can gang dag sangs rgyas kyi ye shes tshol ba dang /sangs rgyas kyi stobs dang /mi 'jigs pa tshol ba'i sems can 
8b3(dkon brtsegs): de lta bu de dag gis/de dag gang la brten nas/dge ba'i rtsa ba rnams skye bar 'gyur ba'i chos gang yang mi dmigs so//'od srung byang chub sems dpa' ji snyed pa 'di dag bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub 
8b4(dkon brtsegs): tshol zhing /sangs rgyas kyi chos rnams las 'dus ma byas gzhan par sems shing /'dus ma byas las sangs rgyas kyi chos gzhan par sems la/sangs rgyas kyi chos rnamasal sems shing /'du shes la 
8b5(dkon brtsegs): spyod par 'gyur ba'am/sangs rgyas kyi chos rnamsla sems pa'o/snyam du shes na/shes pa de sangs rgyas kyi chos rnams la phyir log pa yin te/de dag ni bla na med pa yang dag bar rdzogs pa'i byang chub 
8b6(dkon brtsegs): la yang dag par zhugs pa zhes mi bya'o//de ci'i phyir zhe na/sangs rgyas kyi chos su sems na/de bdag tu 'dzin pa nyid yin pa'i phyir ro//nyin gcig bzhin du sbyor bas/de la brtson par byed na/
8b7(dkon brtsegs): de ni de'i bdag tu 'dzin pa yin te 'bral ba ma yin no//gang ma bral ba de sems pa'o//gang sems pa dang /gang gis sems pa dang /gang la sems pa de rnam par nyams pa'o//gang rnam par 
8b8(dkon brtsegs): nyams pa de rnam par rgyug go//gang rnam par rgyug pa de kun tu rgyug go//gang kun tu rgyug pa de rjes su 'gro'o//gang rjes su 'gro ba de rtog go//gang rtog pa de rnam par rtog go//gang rnam par
9a1(dkon brtsegs): rtog pa de rab tu rtog go/gang rab tu rtog pa de kun rtog go/gang kun tu rtog pa de ma zhi ba'o//gang ma zhi ba de g.yon phyogs pa'o//gang g.yon phyogs pa de 'khyam pa'o//gang 'khyam 
9a2(dkon brtsegs): pa de 'phongs pa'o//cis 'phongs zhe na bde bas 'phongs so//bde ba gang zhe na/gang la kun tu rtog pa med pa de bde ba'o//gang phongs pa de skyon can no//skyon can gang yin pa de nye bar 'gro ba'o//gang nye bar 
9a3(dkon brtsegs): 'gro ba de bag la nyal ba'o//bag la nyal ba gang yin pa de mtshams sbyor ba'o//gang mtshams sbyor ba de rnam par mtshams sbyor ba'o//gang rnam par mtshams sbyor ba de yongs su mtshams sbyor ba'o//gang yongs su 
9a4(dkon brtsegs): mtshams sbyor ba de rnam pa smra ba'o//gang rnam par smra ba de smra ba lkugs pa'o//gang smra ba lkugs pa de mya ngan byed pa'o//gang mya ngan byed pa de tshig par byed pa'o//gang tshig par byed pa de nyams pa ste/yid 
9a5(dkon brtsegs): du 'ong ba dang /yid du mi 'ong ba'i chos shes gang la gnas pa'i dra ba can no//'di la chos gang yang gnas pa med mod kyi/'on kyang 'du shes kyi rgyun gyis 'du shes bcings shing /'du shes kyi bcings pas/
9a6(dkon brtsegs): 'du shes kyi rgyun te/des na 'du shes kyis bcings pa zhes bya'o//ma byung ba'i gnas ni 'dod chags kyi gnas/khro ba'i gnas/zhe sdang gi gnas/gti mug gi gnas te/yang dag pa ma yin pa'i 
9a7(dkon brtsegs): kun rtog pa la rtog rnam par rtog/dpyod rnam par dpyod pas de'i phyir/de bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub myur du mi 'thob bo//'od srung de ltar na sred pa'i gnas te/ci'i phyir sred pa'i gnas shes bya 
9a8(dkon brtsegs): zhe na ba'o//gang sreg pa zhes bya ba'i chos de yang gang yang med la/'di la gang gis sred pa'i chos gang yang med cing /de la gang la sred pa'i chos kyang gang yang med la'i phyir ro//'on kyang sred pa 
9b1(dkon brtsegs): zhes mngon par zhen pas mngon par zhen te/nam mkha' la sbag par byed do//gang nam mkha' la sbag par byed/bden pa rnyed par 'gyur te/bdag gis sbags pa dang /sems can gyis sbags

9b8(dkon brtsegs): du rgyug go/gang sdug bsngal dang /sdug sngal la kun du rgyug pa de/smra ba la rgyug cing gnas pa'o zhes de bzhin gshegs pas gsungs so//ci'i phyir smra ba la rgyug cing gnas pa zhes bya zhe na/shin du yid 
10a1(dkon brtsegs): @#//la byed pa'i phyir ro//gang la yid la byed pa yod pa de la ngar 'dzin pa yod do//gang la ngar 'dzin pa yod pa de la byed pa yod do//gang la byed pa yod pa de la shin tu yod do//gang la shin 
10a2(dkon brtsegs): tu yod pa de kun tu brjod pa dang /nye bar brjod pa dang /gnas par byed pa'o zhes/de bzhin gshegs pas gsungs te/de'i phyir chos thams cad ni yid la byed pa las byung ba'o//'od srung 'di lta ste/
10a3(dkon brtsegs): dper na/steng gi bar snang las sprin gyi phung po byung ba de dag ni/shar phyogs nas ma 'ongs/lho phyogs nas ma yin/nub phyogs nas ma yin/byang phyogs nas ma yin/steng nas ma yin/'og nas 
10a4(dkon brtsegs): ma yin/phyogs nas ma yin/phyogs mtshams nas kyang ma 'ongs te/de bzhin gshegs pa yang dag par gsung bas/de phyogs bcu nas ma 'ongs par mkhyen nas/don gyi tshul dang /rigs pa'i tshul dang /
10a5(dkon brtsegs): yang dag pa'i tshul gyis sprin gyi phung po ni/phung po med pa'o zhes yang dag pa ji lta ba bzhin du gsungs te/des na phung po med pa ni sprin gyi phung po zhes bya'o//ci'i phyir sprin gyi phung po zhes bya zhe na/yangs

10a8(dkon brtsegs): yal zhig gis mi gzhan zhig la 'di skad ces smras so//grib ma der dong la 'khod par bya'o//de nas mkhas pa'i rang bzhin can gyis mi des 'di skad ces smras te/kho bo ni spro ba med de 'di na 'dug go/

10b4(dkon brtsegs): dam pa'o//de ci'i phyir zhe na/de bzhin gshegs pas mkhyen la de bzhin gshegs pas ma mkhyen pa'i phyir te/sems can gyi 'du shes gang yin pa de 'di 'du shes med pa'o//de la byis pa'i rang

11a1(dkon brtsegs): rten 'di na gnas pa de dag ni brkam pa'o//gang dag brkam pa de dag khro ba'o//gang dag khro ba de dag rmongs pa'o//gang dag rmongs pa de dag ma dag pa'o//gang dag ma dag pa de dag 'gal ba'o//

12a1(dkon brtsegs): par 'gyur ba dang /dag par 'gyur ba dang /rnam par dag par 'gyur ba dang /yongs su dag par 'gyur ba dang /rnam par gnon par 'gyur ba'i skabs yod do//de dag chos kyang ston te/chos de dag 
12a2(dkon brtsegs): nam mkha' ltar byas kyi/chos de dag phung por ma byas so//de dag rigs pa rnams la chos chen po ston te/mi rigs pa rnams la ma yin no//yon tan dang ldan pa rnams la ste/nyes par gnas pa rnams

12a6(dkon brtsegs): 'gyur/dge slong ma sun par byed par mi 'gyur/khyim gyi nang du 'dre bar mi 'gyur/srog gcod par mi 'gyur/ma byin par len par mi 'gyur/'dod pa la log par g.yem par mi 'gyur/brdzun smra

12b1(dkon brtsegs): ma ning dang /sten par mi byed/smad 'tshong ma spyod yul par mi 'gyur/yugs mo spyod yul par mi 'gyur/na chung chen mo spyod yul par mi 'gyur /bya ba dang 'dre bar mi 'gyur/nya ba dang 'dre bar mi 'gyur /ri dgas kyi rngon pa

110b2(dkon brtsegs): de phyir bla na med pa'i chos//'di kun mkhyen cing gzigs pa yin//ngas ni khyod dang sems can gyi//tshad gzung med cing mtha' yas pa'i//don chen byas te de las ni//gzungs 'di thob par 'gyur ba yin//

110b4(dkon brtsegs): med pa ste//nga yi ye shes phung po des//chos 'di rab tu bshad pa mdzad//shes rab ldan pa gang zhig ni//byang chub ched du rtogs 'dod pa//'jigs med de yis don tshul 'dir//
110b5(dkon brtsegs): yang dag nyid du brtson par gyis//mkhas pa gang zhig shes rab kyi//snang ba mang po 'dod pa des//sangs rgyas bsten bkur rab byas nas//tshul 'di dag la slab par gyis//chos kyi 'khor lo bskor 
110b6(dkon brtsegs): ba dang //chos dung bud par 'dod pa gang //shes rab ldan pa de yis ni//bstan pa 'di la bslab par gyis//mi gang 'od zer mtha' yas pa//bkye bar 'dod cing sangs rgyas kyi//chos don 
110b7(dkon brtsegs): gnyer ba gang yin des//'di la tshul bzhin bslab par gyis//dam pa gang zhig lhar bcas pa'i//'jig rten zil gnon 'dod pa des//chos kun rnam par nges pa yi//mdo 'di la ni bslab par 
110b8(dkon brtsegs): gyis//nga yis ye shes gzhal du med//ye shes brtson pa tshad med de//sangs rgyas ye shes 'dod pa gang //de yis 'di la rjes su slobs//sangs rgyas ye shes spros med cing //ye shes 
111a1(dkon brtsegs): dam par gyur pa'i tshul//thams cad rab tu ston 'dod gang //de yis don 'di bslab par gyis//gang zhig ye shes chags med pa//ston pa rab tu 'chad 'dod pa//de dag gis ni chos

111a4(dkon brtsegs): shin tu rgyas pa 'dir bshad pa//byang chub sems dpa' rnams la bstan//bkod pa mtha' yas//de lta bas na byang chub sems dpa' rnamasakyis/sems can rnams la phan gdags pa'i ched du/

111a6(dkon brtsegs): mtha' yas/gang dag ma 'ongs pa'i dus na//chos 'di thos nas dga' ba dang rab tu dga' ba dag/ldan pa'i sems kyis/de bzhin gshegs pa yid la byed par 'gyur ba de dag gis ni/de bzhin gshegs pa 
111a7(dkon brtsegs): rnams kyi chos kyi gter mtha' yas pa 'di yang thob par 'gyur la/spobs pa phun sum tshogs pa yang 'dzin par 'gyur/myur du chos thams cad la dbang sgyur ba yang thob par 'gyur/
111a8(dkon brtsegs): sangs rgyas kyi zhing phun sum tshogs pa dang /nyan thos phun sum tshogs pa dang /byang chub sems dpa' phun sum tshogs pa bsam gyis mi khyab pa yang 'dzin par 'gyur ro//bkod pa

112a1(dkon brtsegs): du gnas par bya ba'i phyir gus pa dang bcas/zhes dang bcas par bya ste/glegs bam la bris nas/byang chub la yang dag par zhugs pa/don du gnyer zhing 'dun pa rnams la rab tu 
112a2(dkon brtsegs): bstan par bya/kun tu bgo bar bya zhing lung dbog pa dang /kha ton bya ba dang /yi ger bri ba dang /don nges par bstan pa'i bar du yang chos yang dag bgro ba la bcab pa med par/chos kyi sgo ji ltar kun

112a4(dkon brtsegs): de ltar zhugs pa'i byang chub sems dpas ni chos gang yang sgrub par mi 'gyur ba dang /don gang rtogs par mi 'gyur ba de chung zad kyang med de/chos kyi sgo gang 'dir ma brjod pa de ni 'ga' yang med kyis//bkod 
112a5(dkon brtsegs): pa mtha' yas/sems can thams cad la phan pa dang bde bar bya ba'i phyir/gzungs kyi sgo rnam par sbyong ba'i chos rim par phye ba 'di zung shig/de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis tshe dang ldan pa kun dga' bo 
112a6(dkon brtsegs): la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to//kun dga' bo chos kyi sgo'i le'u 'di zung shig/gsol pa bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa'i mthus 'di bdag gis bzung lags te/bcom ldan 'das chos kyi 
112a7(dkon brtsegs): sgo 'di dang ldan pa ni chos kyi sgo tshad med pa mngon du 'gyur ba lags so//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/kun dga' bo de de bzhin te/de bzhin gshegs pa'i mthu chos kyi sgo 'di la dad pa 
112a8(dkon brtsegs): dang /chos kyi sgo 'di kun 'dzin pa dang /bsnyen bkur byed pa rnams la chos kyi sgo mang po mngon du 'gyur ro//kun dga' bo de'i phyir khyod kyis chos kyi sgo/de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi chos mang po'i

112b6(dkon brtsegs): dga' bo gang dag 'di la sems dang po bskyed nas bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub la yang dag pa zhugs pa de dag thams cad ni bskal pa grangs med pa dag gis bla na med pa yang dag 
112b7(dkon brtsegs): par rdzogs pa'i byang chub mngon par rdzogs par 'tshang rgya bar 'gyur zhing /spobs pa mtha' yas pa nyid la yang nges par 'byung bar 'gyur ro//de nas de'i tshe bcom ldan 'das kyis gzungs kyi phyag rgya

307b4(dkon brtsegs): ba dang /bsnyen bkur bya ba' phyir bde ba can du skye bar /dge ba'i rtsa ba rnams yongs su sngo bar byed do//de dag thams cad der skyes nas kyang /mthar gyis byang chub sems dpa'i 
307b5(dkon brtsegs): spyod pa yongs su rdzogs par byas te /sangs rgyas kyi zhing gzhan dag tu   de bzhin gshegs pa 'jam pa'i dbyangs shes bya bar bla na med pa yang dag (par) rdzogs pa'i byang chub mngon par
307b6(dkon brtsegs): rdzogs par 'tshang rgya bar 'gyur ro//de bzhin gshegs pa mar ma mdzad las bzod pa thob ste /bla na med pa yang dag parardzogs pa'i byang chub las phyir mi ldog pa bye ba khrag khrig 
307b7(dkon brtsegs): phrag brgyad cu de bzhin gshegs pa 'od pag med kyis sngon byang chub sems dpa'i spyad pa spyod pa na bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub du yongs su smin par byas pa de dag kyang /'jig 
307b8(dkon brtsegs): 'arten gyi khams bde ba can du skyes te /sngon gyi smon lam dang /spyod pa yongs su rdzogs para'agyur ro /de'i tshe stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams 'di rnam pa 
308a1(dkon brtsegs): drug tu g.yos te/cho 'phrul rnam pa sna tshogs kyang kun du snang ngo //me tog mAn+ta ra ba dag kyang sal pus mo nub tsam du bkram par gyur to//lha dang mi'i sil snyan rnams 
308a2(dkon brtsegs): kyang rab tu grags par gyur to//'og min gyi gnas kyi bar du yang rjes su yi rang ngo zhes bya ba'i sgra grags par gyur to//bcom ldan 'das kyis de skad ces bka' stsal nas/byang chub sems dpa' 
308a3(dkon brtsegs): sems dpa' chen po mi pham pa dang /tshe dang ldan pa kun dga' bo dang /thams cad dang ldan pa'i 'khor de dang /lha dang mi dang /lha ma yin dang /dri zar bcas pa'i 'jig rten yid rangs te/
308a4(dkon brtsegs): bcom ldan 'das kyis gsungs pa la mngon par bstod do//'phags pa dkon mchog brtsegs pa chen po'i chos kyi rnam grangs le'u stong phrag brgya ba las/de bzhin gshegs pa 'od dpag tu med 
308a5(dkon brtsegs): pa'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing gi yon tan bkod pa zhes bya ba ste/le'u lnga pa brdzogs so////ban de klu'i rgyal mtshan gyis bsgyur cing zhus//dge'o//
